<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000977 -->
# Exact six/eight-anchor multistage completions and two special word families

All norms and inner products are Euclidean. For an integer n>=0, a finite
nonnegative physical schedule h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)), and L>0, let W_n(h)
be the supremum of

    2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2)

over every finite-dimensional convex differentiable f with L-Lipschitz
gradient, every minimizer x_star, and x_0!=x_star, using the actual updates
x_(i+1)=x_i-(h_i/L)grad f(x_i). Write R_n(h)=W_n(h)/2. The witnesses
below use smoothness1 and the true closest initial distance to the complete
minimum set. Multiplication of their objective by any L>0 gives the same
points with steps h_i/L and the same normalized payoff.

## Definitions of the two completed functions

Fix real parameters M=M_mc>=2, B=B_mc>=2, A=A_mc>=4B+4, and
epsilon in [0,1]. These are local construction parameters, distinct from
the benchmark sequences C_n and B_n below. Put a=a_mc=1-1/M,
C=C_mc=2B+1, and let e_1,e_2,e_3 be the standard basis of R^3. Define

    nu=sqrt(2A-4),
    p=p_mc=e_1/M,
    q=q_mc=(-2e_1+nu e_2)/M,
    Q=Qnorm_mc=||q||^2=2A/M^2,
    r=r_mc=-q/2+sqrt((B/2-1/4)Q)e_3,
    R=Rnorm_mc=||r||^2=BQ/2,       <q,r>=-Q/2,
    D_mc=conv{0,p,q,r},
    F_old(x)=max_(g in D_mc){<x,g>-||g||^2/2},
    y=y_mc=-Ae_1-Mq,
    z=z_mc=y-BMq-Mr,
    w=w_mc=z-CMr-Mq,
    t_P=a e_1,              delta_P=a/M,
    t_Q=y+(M-1)q,           delta_Q=(M-1)Q,
    t_R=z+(M-1)r,           delta_R=(M-1)R,
    t_D=w+(M-1)q,           delta_D=(M-1)Q.

Here conv denotes convex hull. For each ordinary list L_i, choose any
finite nonnegative list of total mass M-1 and retain every entry, including
zeros. With concatenation in the displayed physical order, define

    h_three=(L_0,AM+1,L_1,BM+1,L_2,1+M epsilon),
    h_return=(L_0,AM+1,L_1,BM+1,L_2,CM+1,L_3,1+M epsilon).

Their physical lengths are sum_(i=0)^2 length(L_i)+3 and
sum_(i=0)^3 length(L_i)+4. Assertions about h_return additionally assume
M epsilon>=2B^2. Define, for v,x in R^3,

    H_three(v)=||v||^2/2+max{0,<t_P,v>-delta_P,
               <y,v>,<t_Q,v>-delta_Q,<z,v>,<t_R,v>-delta_R},
    H_return(v)=||v||^2/2+max{0,<t_P,v>-delta_P,
               <y,v>,<t_Q,v>-delta_Q,<z,v>,<t_R,v>-delta_R,
               <w,v>,<t_D,v>-delta_D},
    F_three(x)=sup_v{<x,v>-H_three(v)},
    F_return(x)=sup_v{<x,v>-H_return(v)}.

These are one fixed globally defined function per full physical run,
specified before that run. To define its complete old prefix, run F_old
from x_0=e_1 on the appropriate schedule and retain every triple
(x_i,grad F_old(x_i),F_old(x_i)) with 0<=i<n, together with the
zero optimum triple. The terminal index n is unqueried by the updates.
For any of the resulting actual functions, its signed ordered residual is
Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2 on
{star,0,...,n}, where f_i=F(x_i), g_i=grad F(x_i), and
x_star=g_star=f_star=0.

## The specifically defined collapsed comparison

For the SAME h_three or h_return, retain the first list L_0 and bridge
AM+1, and treat all subsequent entries before the last 1+M epsilon as
one ordinary list, retaining their physical indices. Its mass is M_2-1,
where

    M_2=M_col_three=(B+2)M,
    or M_2=M_col_return=(B+C+3)M=(3B+4)M.

Set q_col=(-2e_1+nu e_2)/M_2 and define the static old collapsed function

    F_col_old(x)=max_(g in conv{0,p,q_col}){<x,g>-||g||^2/2}.

Run F_col_old from e_1 on that same full physical word and retain all
its old queried triples at indices 0<=i<n.
The collapsed completion class consists exactly of all everywhere defined
convex differentiable 1-smooth functions on R^3 interpolating its ENTIRE
old queried prefix, including values and gradients, and the optimum triple
(0,0,0). It is nonempty. For any such completion F_col, the same actual word
from e_1 has normalized true-distance payoff V_col(F_col). Define the positive
or zero upper comparison number

    U_col_three=2A epsilon^2/(B+2)^2,
    U_col_return=2A epsilon^2/(3B+4)^2,
    rho_col_three=[B(B+2)^2/2](1-1/(2B^3)),
    rho_col_return=(3/4)(3B+4)^2.

## Exact named reference channels and original benchmark

For any h of length n, put s(h)=sum_(i<n)h_i, P_j=sum_(i<j)h_i,
T_j=sum_(i>j)h_i, and (u)_+=max(u,0). The accepted single-cut channel is

    K_n(h)=max{1/(1+2s(h)),
          max_(0<=j<n)(h_j-1)_+^2/[(1+P_j)^2(1+2T_j)]},

with the inner maximum omitted at n=0. Define the entire convex-quadratic
channel by

    Q_n(h)=max_(0<=t<=1) t product_(i<n)(1-h_i t)^2.

This is the supremum of the normalized payoff over all finite-dimensional
convex quadratics with gradient Lipschitz constant at most L, with arbitrary
initial points and chosen minimizers, in the original normalization.

For n>=1 and a complex variable zeta=alpha+i beta, put
Poly_0(zeta)=1, Poly_i(zeta)=product_(t<i)(1-h_t zeta),
xi_i=Poly_i(zeta), gamma_i=zeta Poly_i(zeta), and xi_star=gamma_star=0.
Identify the complex plane with the real plane using <u,v>=Re(conj(u)v). On the index set
I_n={star,0,...,n}, the weight of the directed edge j->i is

    q_ij=<gamma_j,xi_i-xi_j>+||gamma_i-gamma_j||^2/2.

Let ell_n be the maximum sum of these weights on directed simple paths
n->star. Define Gap_h(zeta)=-2ell_n for zeta in the exact universal lens

    Lens_n={alpha+i beta:
       alpha-alpha^2-beta^2>=|beta|cot(pi/(n+2))},
    L_lens_n(h)=sup_(zeta in Lens_n) Gap_h(zeta),
    V_lens(h)=max{L_lens_n(h),1/(1+2s(h))}.

The meaning and legality of this transcript are those of accepted fact_id
dfde2fa326c908bf; the reference is restricted to Lens_n. No upper
comparison with the full schedule-dependent complex legal region is made.

Use the original all-split sequences, exactly as in accepted fact_id
b98bb807d514e80d. The joins have real arguments x,y>=0, and the
recurrences apply for every integer n>=1:

    C_0=B_0=1,
    Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2,
    G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2,
    C_n=max_(0<=j<n) Omega(C_j,C_(n-j-1)),
    B_n=max_(0<=j<n) G(C_j,B_(n-j-1)),
    U_n^ZJ=1/B_n,      lambda=1+sqrt(2),      Kc=cosh(2).

Every maximum here ranges over ALL integer splits, with C_n=S_circle(n)+1
and B_n=2S_bullet(n)+1 in that predecessor's notation.

For each integer N>=2^60, set

    M=2N(N+1)+1, A=M^(1/12), B=M^(1/120), epsilon=M^(-1/3),
    T_Cheb=(2N+1)^2=2M-1.

Let Chi_N be the list of entries
sec^2((2j-1)pi/[2(2N+1)]), 1<=j<=N. Let L_up be three consecutive
increasing copies of Chi_N and L_down three consecutive decreasing copies.
Use L_0=L_up and L_i=L_down for every subsequent i. Thus the two base
word lengths are 9N+3 and 12N+4. For every integer n>=9*2^60+3 define
the padded three-stage word by N=floor((n-3)/9), followed by inserting
n-(9N+3) leading zero steps into that base word. For every integer
n>=12*2^60+4 define the padded return word by N=floor((n-4)/12) and
insert n-(12N+4) leading zero steps. These are the two SPECIAL families
in the conclusions below, with their respective N and parameters.

## Conclusions

(i) F_three and, on its additional parameter domain, F_return are finite
everywhere, convex, continuously differentiable, and have least global
gradient Lipschitz constant EXACTLY1. Each is the pointwise maximal legal
completion of its complete specified old prefix. The exact maximum of
all physical prefix conjugate quadratics is H_three or H_return, with
six or eight distinct affine anchors, respectively; the first r-query
anchor t_R is retained in BOTH laws. Every old prefix point, value and
gradient is preserved. The actual queried gradients are p,q,r for the
three-stage law and p,q,r,q for the return law, one constant gradient on
each ordinary list and its following bridge query. Every signed ordered
interpolation residual, including every remote, optimum and terminal pair,
in both orientations, is nonpositive for the resulting actual function.
The complete minimum sets and minimum values are

    argmin F_three=conv{0,y,z},   min F_three=0,
    argmin F_return=conv{0,y,z,w},   min F_return=0.

Origin is the unique closest minimizer to e_1, at distance1 in both cases.

(ii) For the three-stage law set

    L_stage=B+a, E_3=M(B+a)q+Mr,
    D_3=||E_3||^2=2A[(B+a)^2-(B+a)+B/2],
    b_3=Aa(epsilon-2/M), theta_3=(b_3)_+/D_3.

The actual terminal point, gradient and normalized payoff are exactly

    x_n=z-M epsilon r,
    grad F_three(x_n)=-M epsilon r-theta_3 E_3,
    V_three=2F_three(x_n)
       =AB epsilon^2-[Aa(epsilon-2/M)_+]^2/D_3,
    AB epsilon^2(1-1/(2B^3))<=V_three<=AB epsilon^2.

This includes epsilon=0, epsilon=2/M when in range, M=2, and epsilon=1.
For the return law set

    d_R=C+a, E_R=Mq+M d_R r,
    Dhat_R=1-d_R+(B/2)d_R^2, D_R=||E_R||^2=2A Dhat_R,
    b_R=2A[(B-1/(2M))epsilon-Ba/(2M)], theta_R=b_R/D_R.

The actual terminal quantities are exactly

    x_n=w-M epsilon q,
    grad F_return(x_n)=-M epsilon q-theta_R E_R,
    V_return=2F_return(x_n)=2A epsilon^2-b_R^2/D_R,
    2A epsilon^2(1-1/(2B))<=V_return<=2A epsilon^2.

They include the feasible boundary M epsilon=2B^2. The denominator
bounds are 2B^3<=Dhat_R<=(9/2)B^3; the lower-point identity is exactly
1-(2B+1)+(B/2)(2B+1)^2=2B^3+2B^2-3B/2.
In particular W_n(h_three)>=V_three and W_n(h_return)>=V_return.

(iii) Every member of the narrowly defined collapsed completion class
has true closest initial radius1 and 0<=V_col(F_col)<=U_col for its word.
On the entire respective parameter domains the WEAK comparisons are

    V_three>=rho_col_three U_col_three>=rho_col_three V_col(F_col),
    V_return>=rho_col_return U_col_return>=rho_col_return V_col(F_col).

At epsilon=0 in the three-stage domain, V_three=U_col_three=V_col(F_col)=0:
the displayed weak comparison has equality and no payoff ratio is asserted.
When epsilon>0 the comparison upper U_col is positive and the ratios
V_three/U_col_three>=rho_col_three>1 and
V_return/U_col_return>=rho_col_return>1 are defined and give strict
domination of every such collapsed completion. Epsilon>0 is automatic
in the return parameter domain. Both factors diverge on their specified
growing families. The comparison covers this exact retained prefix and
second slope, with no claim about other partitions, slopes, or witnesses.

(iv) For each of the two base growing words and each of its padded words,
at the ACTUAL physical horizon n, all the following bounds hold:

    V_three>=(7/8)M^(-23/40),
    V_return>=(3/2)M^(-7/12),
    V>=(3/2)M^(-7/12)  for either law,
    K_n(h)<=41M^(-5/6),
    Q_n(h_three)<=64M^(-49/60),
    Q_n(h_return)<=1600M^(-4/5),
    L_lens_n(h_three)<=128Kc^18 M^(-49/60),
    L_lens_n(h_return)<=3200Kc^24 M^(-4/5).

Writing d_N=floor(log_2 N), the actual original benchmark obeys

    B_n V >= [3lambda^3/(2*20^(7/12))]
                       (lambda/2^(7/6))^(d_N)>1.

Consequently each SPECIAL family supplies an attained static witness at
EVERY integer horizon above its stated threshold with V>U_n^ZJ, and
V/U_n^ZJ tends to infinity. Meanwhile

    max{K_n(h),Q_n(h),L_lens_n(h)}=o(U_n^ZJ)

along each family, also with V_lens in place of L_lens_n. These results
neither upper-bound unrestricted W_n on these words nor quantify over
arbitrary words. They give no comparison with all nonlinear scalar
functions, all earlier adversaries, or the full complex legal region.
The original arbitrary-word asymptotic exact-profile theorem remains OPEN.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000978 -->
# Full local seven-step geometry: base rigidity and the remaining cone

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. Worker max2, ONE round
after completed46, under TASK.md and master_guidance f8408daf213a461d;
plan d1e013ea01c97469. No fact submission is authorized or made. Neither
an integrating ascent certificate nor a neighborhood capacity bound is
proved. The original Goal remains OPEN.

The new calculation covers EVERY base certificate: actual equality
witnesses confine its remainder to one ray; an original multiplier sign
eliminates the ray; the entire base array is then unique. Uniqueness is
derived, not assumed. Six necessary physical tangent inequalities follow.
They leave a strict positive-capacity direction. The full original
sign/PSD linearization, an attempted completion, and the exact quadratic
correction are given below. Their unresolved feasibility is distinguished
from the contact test.

Accepted inputs: 04219554e41d9750, proof Sections10--11, for the original
primitive identity and composition; 1252b8823bb5a290 for interpolation
orientation; 5e1848ee0b153f5f for the conditional length-seven interface
A>lambda^3. Earlier drafts, including 8ed2e5206744ba63 and ff8b941536cb035e,
are awareness only. Every additional result used below is proved jointly
in this UNVERIFIED manuscript.

## 1. Original objects and a supplied base array

Put q=sqrt(2), lambda=1+q, u=q-1=1/lambda, and

 hbar=(q,2,q,1+lambda,q,2,q), Abar=lambda^3.

For a nearby nonnegative word h of length7 set s=sum h_i, A=1+s. Retain
all eight independent formal gradients. For a zero-diagonal array Lambda,

 e=(1,...,1)^T in R8,
 T(h)_ii=1, T(h)_ij=h_j for j<i, T(h)_ij=0 for j>i,
 L(Lambda)=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega(h)=(-2h_0,...,-2h_6,2s)^T,
 D(h)=diag(h_0(2-h_0),...,h_6(2-h_6),-s(s+2)),
 M(h,Lambda)=D(h)+sym(L(Lambda)T(h)), sym(X)=(X+X^T)/2.       (1)

Also skew(X)=(X-X^T)/2 throughout.

T(h) here is always the prefix matrix, not an older single-cut variable.
The original conditions are ALL56 off-diagonal signs Lambda_ij>=0,
L^T e=omega, and M>=0 on the ENTIRE R8. On the original positions
x_i=x_0-sum_(k<i)h_k g_k, put

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<7)h_i(f_i-f_7)
       +sum_(i<7)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_7||^2.

Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives the formal identity

 V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_7)>.                (2)

The identity requires flow, not legality of the jets. Actual smooth
convex jets have Q_ij<=0.

One complete base array Lambdabar has these TWENTY positive entries.
All other off-diagonal entries and its diagonal are zero.

 ij | Lambdabar_ij
 ---|--------------------------------
 01 | 2
 10 | 2lambda
 13 | 2lambda
 21 | 2lambda q
 23 | 2lambda^2+2lambda q
 31 | 2lambda
 32 | 2lambda^3
 37 | 2lambda^2
 43 | 2lambda q
 45 | 2lambda^2
 47 | 2lambda q
 53 | 4lambda
 54 | 2lambda^3
 57 | 2lambda^3+4lambda
 63 | 2lambda q
 65 | 2lambda^3 q
 67 | 2lambda^4+2lambda^3 q+2lambda q
 73 | 2lambda
 75 | 2lambda^3
 76 | 2lambda^5

Here is its exact derivation. The singleton q has multipliers2,2lambda.
Its LT has diagonal(-2u,2lambda) and off-diagonal(-2,2); D has diagonal
(2u,-2lambda), so M=0. Joining two singletons uses bridge2, capacity
lambda^2, right scaling kappa=lambda^2 and extra factor kappa-1=2lambda.
The accepted join identity gives the first seven entries in the table
on local indices0,...,3. Joining two of these children uses bridge
1+lambda, the same scaling and extra factor, cross entries
37:2lambda^2,43:2lambda q,53:4lambda,63:2lambda q,73:2lambda, and adds
2lambda*h_i to i7 for i=4,5,6. Scaling the right child gives the table.
Thus exact flow and M(hbar,Lambdabar)=0 follow from the accepted full
composition identity. This supplies existence, not yet uniqueness.

## 2. Actual static equality witnesses

For a compact convex K containing0 define

 F_K(x)=max_(g in K){<x,g>-||g||^2/2}.                        (3)

Completing the square gives grad F_K(x)=P_K(x). A proposed g is the
projection iff g in K and <x-g,z-g><=0 for every z in K. Adding the
normal inequalities at two projections proves firm nonexpansiveness
and hence 1-Lipschitzness. The two maximizing inequalities in(3) bound
the error after its linear term by ||z||^2 under displacement z, proving
differentiability. A supremum of affine functions is convex. If0 is
interior to K, a small positive multiple of each nonzero x in K gives
F_K(x)>0, so the unique minimizer is0.

At actual queries, put y_i=x_i-g_i and
f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2. Direct expansion gives EVERY directed row,
including both optimum orientations,

 Q_ij=<y_j,g_i-g_j>.                                        (4)

A normal check against all vertices therefore checks all56 finite rows
and all optimum rows. The GD norm telescope gives

 V_h=||x_0||^2-||x_7+s g_7||^2.                             (5)

Equation(5) also holds for formal data satisfying GD and the displayed
value formula, regardless of whether their gradients are projections.

(a) A scalar Huber function started sufficiently far into its positive
linear part supplies the gradient column e and V=Q=0. The unit quadratic
started at1 supplies

 vbar=(1,-u,u,-u^2,u,-u^2,u^2,-u^3)^T,                       (6)

also V=Q=0. For any h, v=T(h)^(-1)e obeys
v_0=1,v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i, and this quadratic has energy

 chi(h)=1-A^2 v_7^2.                                       (7)

This follows by telescoping h_i(2-h_i)v_i^2=v_i^2-v_(i+1)^2.

(b) For K=[-u,1], start at lambda^2. All prefix gradients through3
are1; x3=1; x4=-lambda. Every right gradient is -u and x7=-u.
These are projections onto one fixed interval, with gradient column

 cbar=(1,1,1,1,-u,-u,-u,-u)^T.                              (8)

The true initial distance is lambda^2, the unique minimum is0, and
V=lambda^4-lambda^6 u^2=0.

(c) Root quadrilateral. Fix theta in(-1,1), let ||U||=||V||=1 and
U dot V=theta, and put G=-(lambda U+V)/q,
K=conv{U,G,V,-V}. Start at lambda^2 U. Actual gradients are

 U,U,U,U,G,V,-V,uV.                                        (9)

Prefix positions through3 are the remaining prefix sums plus1, times U;
x4=-lambda U,x5=V,x6=-V,x7=uV. Every GD equality holds. Only the prefix
normals at0,1,2 and y4=(-U+V)/q can be nonzero. Prefix support follows
from U dot G=-(lambda+theta)/q<1 and |theta|<1. At4,

 y4 dot(V-G)=0,
 y4 dot(U-G)=2(theta-1)/q<0,
 y4 dot(-V-G)=-2(1-theta)/q<0.

All other normals vanish. The positive relation lambda U+qG+V=0 puts0
inside a triangle in K. Formula(4) checks every row, the true radius is
lambda^2, and(5) gives V=0. Its two coordinate columns span

 aR=(1,1,1,1,-lambda/q,0,0,0)^T,
 bR=(0,0,0,0,-1/q,1,-1,u)^T.                               (10)

(d) Left quadrilateral WITH the original right block. Fix theta in(-1,1),
let ||U||=1,||V||=u,U dot V=u theta, and put
G=-(U+V)/q,K=conv{U,G,V,-lambda V}. Start at lambda U. The data are

 g=(U,U,G,V,-lambda V,V,-V,uV),
 x1=U,x2=-U,x3=V,x4=-lambda V,x5=V,x6=-V,x7=uV.              (11)

Only y0=qU and y2=(-uU+V)/q can be nonzero. Write k=U dot V.
The prefix normal supports all vertices since -u<k<u,
U dot G=-(1+k)/q<1 and -lambda k<1. At2,

 y2 dot(V-G)=0,
 y2 dot(U-G)=k-u<0,
 y2 dot(-lambda V-G)=-(lambda+1)(u^2-u k)/q<0.

The first equality uses u^2+2u=1. The remaining queries have zero normal
and belong to K, including both occurrences of V. The relation U+qG+V=0
puts0 in its interior. All rows follow from(4), the true radius is lambda,
and V=lambda^2-lambda^6 u^4=0. Coordinate columns span

 aL=(1,1,-1/q,0,0,0,0,0)^T,
 bL=(0,0,-1/q,1,-lambda,1,-1,u)^T.                          (12)

Repeated ACTUAL gradients in these witnesses identify no formal
certificate coordinates.

## 3. A single possible remainder ray for EVERY base array

On any equality witness, identity(2) for any feasible array has a
nonpositive left side and a nonnegative right side. Both are zero.
Every strict residual forces its multiplier to vanish. Moreover M
annihilates every coordinate column of the gradient matrix. Indeed a
PSD quadratic with zero value annihilates the vector, as follows by
testing that vector plus t*w for arbitrary real t and w.

Thus M annihilates e,vbar,cbar,aR,bR,aL,bL. Here is the full rank check.
For z orthogonal to those columns, aL and bR give
z0+z1=z2/q and z5-z6+u z7=z4/q. Then bL gives
z3=z2/q+lambda*z4/q; substituting in aR gives lambda*z2=0.
Consequently z2=0,z0+z1=0,z3=lambda*z4/q. The e and cbar equations
force z3=z4=0 and z5+z6+z7=0. Together with bR they give
z5=-z7/q,z6=-u*z7/q. Finally vbar gives q*z0=0.
The common orthogonal complement is exactly the line

 z47=(0,0,0,0,0,-1/q,-u/q,1)^T.

Therefore EVERY feasible base remainder has

 M=tau*z47*z47^T, tau>=0.                                  (13)

Strict rows of the quadrilaterals also force

 Lambda_(i,j)=0 for i=4,5,6,7 and j=0,1,2;
 Lambda_(i,4)=0 for i=0,1,2,3,6,7;
 Lambda_(i,0)=0 for i=2,...,7;
 Lambda_(i,2)=0 for i=0,1,4,6,7.                            (14)

These are consequences, not a chosen support. Unlisted signs remain.

## 4. The original Lambda56 sign eliminates that ray; the base is unique

We reproduce the saturated-cut algebra and retain its possible signed
terminal coefficients. Suppose left/right capacities are a,b, bridge
1+d, d(a+b+d)=ab, C=a+b+d, kappa=(C/b)^2. Let P be left gradient
indices ending at bridge query p, B right indices ending at z.
The clipped equality trajectory has prefix gradient1, right gradient
-eta, eta=d/b=a/C. If R_i^a,R_j^b are the remaining child step sums, (4)
gives its cross residuals

 Q_ij=-(1+eta)eta R_j^b, Q_ji=-(1+eta)R_i^a
                       (i in P,j in B).                   (15)

Positive steps and complementarity leave only cross edges i->z and j->p.
Write their coefficients nu_i,mu_j. The common and clipped gradient
columns span the block indicators, so M kills both. Directly from(1),

 (M e_B)_i=-b*nu_i/2                         (i<p),
 (M e_B)_p=(-b*nu_p+d*sum mu_j)/2,
 (M e_P)_j=(d*mu_j-nu_p*h_j)/2               (p<j<z),
 (M e_P)_z=(d*mu_z-nu_p)/2.                                (16)

To check the first two formulas: T e_B is0 on P and the local right
prefix-capacity vector on B, ending at b. L^T e_B equals -sum mu at p
and omega_j+sum nu*1_(j=z) on B. The block flow is
sum mu-sum nu=2(a+d). Substitution gives the first two rows. For the
last two, T e_P equals a+d on B and a at p; L T e_P has entries d mu_j
on B, and T^T L^T e_P has entries -nu_p h_j and -nu_p at z.

Equations(16) and block flow force

 nu_i=0 (i<p), nu_p=d(kappa-1),
 mu_j=(kappa-1)h_j (j<z), mu_z=kappa-1.                     (17)

In fact sum mu=b*nu_p/d, so nu_p(b/d-1)=2(a+d); dC=ab gives(17).
Subtract these cross terms using the accepted join identity as a formal
identity. The remainder is block diagonal:

 M=M_left+kappa*M_right,
 Lambda^right_ij=Lambda_ij/kappa
                 -(1-1/kappa)h_i*1_(i<z,j=z).             (18)

Both blocks have their own exact flow and are PSD. Left coefficients are
ordinary. Right coefficients can be signed ONLY into z; their other
signs remain nonnegative. This qualification cannot be dropped.

Apply(15)--(18) at the central cut of hbar:
a=b=lambda^2,d=lambda,kappa=lambda^2. All five root cross entries in
Section1 are forced. Equation(13) gives M_left=0 and
M_right=(tau/kappa)*zeta*zeta^T, where zeta=(0,-1/q,-u/q,1)^T on local
right indices0,...,3. Subtract the basic three-step array from that right
array, calling the difference Delta and t=tau/kappa. Its flow is zero.
By(14), Delta20=Delta30=0.

Name the remaining entries

 Delta10=a0, Delta01=b0, Delta02=c0, Delta03=d0,
 Delta12=e0, Delta13=f0, Delta21=g0, Delta23=k0,
 Delta31=l0, Delta32=m0.

For local word(q,2,q), the matrix L(Delta)T is explicitly

 [-u(b0+c0+d0), -b0-2c0-2d0, -c0-q*d0, -d0],
 [u*a0,          a0-e0-f0,   -e0-q*f0, -f0],
 [0,             g0,         g0-u*k0, -k0],
 [0,             l0,         q*l0+u*m0,l0+m0].              (19)

Its symmetric part is t*zeta*zeta^T. Zero row/column0 force
d0=c0=b0=a0=0. Flow, diagonal entries, and entry13 give

 e0+f0=-t/2, l0=f0-q*t,
 g0=(q-1/2)t-f0, m0=lambda*t-f0, k0=t-f0,
 g0-u*k0=t*u^2/2.

The last equality gives f0=t/q. Hence all remaining entries are

 e0=-lambda*t/2, f0=t/q, g0=u*t/2,
 k0=u*t/q, l0=-t/q, m0=lambda*t/q.                         (20)

The other mixed entries check explicitly:
(-e0-q*f0+g0)/2=t*u/2 and
(-k0+q*l0+u*m0)/2=-t*u/q. Thus no equation is missing.
The child's entry12 has basic value0 and equals -lambda*t/2.
It targets a NONTERMINAL index, so(18) requires its nonnegativity.
This forces t=tau=0. Equivalently the original parent entry
Lambda56=-lambda*tau/2 could not be nonnegative for tau>0.

Thus EVERY base remainder is0. At t=0, (19)--(20) also give Delta=0,
so the right child is unique even among the allowed signed-terminal
arrays. For the left child the incoming-first-node mask
Lambda20=Lambda30=0 follows from(14); its remainder is0, and the same
calculation gives uniqueness. With the forced cross coefficients,

 (hbar,Lambda feasible) ==> Lambda=Lambdabar and M=0.        (21)

This UNVERIFIED pointwise base result is not a neighborhood theorem.

## 5. Uniform control of arbitrary nearby certificate selections

Choose a small closed neighborhood with all steps in[1,4].
The scalar quadratic f(x)=k*x^2/2 with k=1/8 and x0=1 has factors
1-k*h_i in[1/2,7/8], so its positions strictly decrease. Every distinct
pair has a uniformly positive separation. Its exact residual is

 Q_ij=-k(1-k)(x_i-x_j)^2/2<0.                              (22)

Compactness and finitely many pairs give -Q_ij>=delta>0 uniformly.
This quadratic's V_h is continuous and bounded above by a finite B.
Identity(2) for any nearby feasible array gives

 delta*sum Lambda_ij <= V_h <= B.                          (23)

Thus ALL such arrays are uniformly bounded. If h_n->hbar, any subsequential
limit of its arrays is feasible at hbar; (21) identifies it as Lambdabar.
A compactness contradiction therefore proves Lambda->Lambdabar uniformly
over arbitrary selections.

Only convergence has been proved. An O(||h-hbar||) rate has NOT been
proved; an unbounded coefficient difference quotient remains possible.

## 6. Six necessary PHYSICAL tangent inequalities

Let p in R7 be any contingent physical direction: t_n>0 tends to0 and
feasible words have h_n=hbar+t_n*p+o(t_n), with arbitrary full arrays.
Write S=sum p_i, ell=p0+p1+p2, r=p4+p5+p6, alpha=u^3.
The following SIX inequalities are necessary:

 JQ=alpha*S+lambda*(p0+p2+p4+p6)+p1+p5+u*p3 <=0,
 JC=alpha*S+u*p3-u^2*(ell+r) <=0,
 JL(1)<=0, JL(-1)<=0, JR(1)<=0, JR(-1)<=0,                  (24)

where

 JL(theta)=alpha*S-u*p0+p1+(1+q*theta)*p2
                       +u*p3+lambda*p4+p5+lambda*p6,
 JR(theta)=alpha*S-u^2*ell+u*p3
                       +(1+q*theta)*p4+p5+lambda*p6.       (25)

Denote the cone defined EXACTLY by these inequalities C_stat47. It is
the exact surviving space of THESE derived contact tests, not asserted
to be the physical feasible tangent cone.

The unit quadratic gives JQ by differentiating log(A*|v7|).
The central cut gives JC: for h3=1+d and side capacities
P=1+h0+h1+h2,R=1+h4+h5+h6, a clipped interval witness starts at P,
ends with magnitude d/R, and has energy P^2-A^2*d^2/R^2.
Thus A*d<=P*R; its logarithmic derivative is JC.

For JR fix theta in(-1,1). Near hbar set
P=1+h0+h1+h2,d=h3-1,a=h4,B=h5-1,C=h6-1, and

 R2=[(a-1)d^2-d(2-a^2)theta]/(a+1),
 U=(1,0), V=(theta,sqrt(R2-theta^2)), G=-(dU+V)/a,
 K=conv{U,G,V,-B*V}.                                      (26)

For sufficiently small changes R2>theta^2 and 0<BC<1. Start at P*U.
Gradients are U through3, then G,V,-B*V,BC*V. All GD equations hold.
Only prefix normals and y4=(-(a-1)dU+V)/a can be nonzero.
The definition of R2 is precisely y4 dot(V-G)=0. Every other nontrivial
vertex comparison is strictly negative at hbar by Section2(c), and stays
so locally for fixed theta. The other normals vanish. This gives an
ACTUAL static function, unique minimum0, all rows, and true radius P.
Its energy is P^2-A^2 B^2 C^2 R2. At the base,

 (R2)'=2u*p3+2(1+q*theta)*p4.

The log derivative of A*B*C*sqrt(R2)/P is JR(theta).
All open theta are allowed, so their endpoint limits give JR(+/-1).
No degenerate endpoint quadrilateral is asserted legal.

For JL the direct continuation need not be an actual trajectory. This
issue is handled explicitly. For fixed theta in(-1,1) put
k=u*theta,d=h1-1,a=h2,P=1+h0 and

 R2=[(a-1)d^2-d(2-a^2)k]/(a+1),
 U=(1,0),V=(k,sqrt(R2-k^2)),G=-(dU+V)/a.                    (27)

Use formal x0=P*U,g0=g1=U,x2=-dU,g2=G,x3=g3=V.
From3 onward set g_i=x_i and x_(i+1)=(1-h_i)x_i.
Set f_i=x_i dot g_i-||g_i||^2/2. These formal jets are analytic near
hbar and are exactly the ACTUAL witness of Section2(d) at the base.
Only y0=h0 U and y2=(-(a-1)dU+V)/a can be nonzero, with
y2 dot(V-G)=0.

EVERY positive Lambdabar entry has Q_ij=0 identically along this formal
continuation. The only positive target0 entry is10, and the only positive
target2 entry is32. All other positive entries target zero normals.
An entry with a strict negative base residual has a nonpositive
contribution Lambda_ij Q_ij near hbar. For a zero base residual with
Lambdabar_ij=0, (21)--(23) give Lambda_ij=o(1) uniformly, while its
analytic residual is O(||h-hbar||). The sum of positive contributions
is therefore o(||h-hbar||). Apply the FORMAL identity(2) and full PSD:

 V_h >= -o(||h-hbar||).                                   (28)

No legality of the continued jets or differentiability of the arrays
was assumed. The right terminal product is
(h3-1)(h4-1)(h5-1)(h6-1), so the formal energy is

 P^2-A^2[(h3-1)(h4-1)(h5-1)(h6-1)]^2 R2.

At the base R2=u^2 and (R2)'/(2u^2)=p1+(1+q*theta)p2.
The first-order limit of(28) gives JL(theta)<=0 and hence its two endpoint
inequalities. Ignoring the return to V at query5 would have falsely
asserted a finite actual-function obstruction for(27).

The other global cuts are strictly slack at hbar. Their exact margins
P_j*R_j-A(h_j-1), for j=0,1,2,4,5,6, are
4+2q,6+4q,18+12q,18+12q,6+4q,4+2q. They add no first-order restriction.
The earlier final-pair defect is also strictly slack here. With
P=1+sum_(i<5)hbar_i=5+4q, B=hbar5-1=1,C=hbar6-1=u,

 H_pair=BC[1+B(P+Abar)/P^2]-1=-4*Abar/P^2<0.                 (29)

Indeed P^2=57+40q and u[P^2+P+Abar]=29+20q.
Its triangle witness has positive energy at hbar, so it supplies no
equality tangent here. Its possible force farther away is not discarded.

## 7. A surviving ascent vector and the ENTIRE lifted first-order problem

The claim that(24) alone excludes ascent is false. Take

 p_test=(0,0,0,0,0,2,-1), S=1.                              (30)

JQ and all four JL/JR endpoint values equal
u^3+2-lambda=u^3-u<0. JC=u^3-u^2<0. Thus p_test lies strictly inside
C_stat47 and has positive capacity derivative. This is a counterexample
to sufficiency of the CONTACT TESTS for a no-ascent conclusion.
It is not a claimed feasible physical path or feasible lifted tangent.

Here is the full lifted first-order system, leaving all seven physical
coordinates independent. Let Y be ANY zero-diagonal real8-by8 array,
F=L(Y), T0=T(hbar), D0=D(hbar), Lbar=L(Lambdabar), and

 T_p,ij=p_j for j<i, zero otherwise,
 omega_p=(-2p0,...,-2p6,2S)^T,
 D1(p)=diag(2(1-hbar0)p0,...,2(1-hbar6)p6,-2*Abar*S),
 N(p)=D1(p)+sym(Lbar*T_p),
 W=N(p)+sym(F*T0).                                         (31)

Original flow, signs and PSD linearize to

 F^T e=omega_p,
 Y_ij>=0 at ALL THIRTY-SIX zero entries of Lambdabar,
 W>=0 on the ENTIRE R8.                                    (32)

The other twenty derivative entries are unrestricted real numbers;
their finite coefficients must still be nonnegative.
Under this flow e^T W e=0; PSD therefore gives W e=0. The other forced
moment is

 vbar^T W vbar=chi1(p)=-2JQ.                                (33)

It follows by differentiating v(h)^T M(h)v(h)=chi(h), using Mbar=0.
Denote the projection of(32) onto p by C_lin47. This is the exact
surviving space for the ORIGINAL LINEARIZED tests. This semidefinite
projection has NOT been solved or eliminated, and equality with the
physical contingent cone is NOT asserted.

A concrete direct completion attempt for(30) goes as follows.
Its flow increment is(0,0,0,0,0,-4,2,2)^T; D1 has diagonal
(0,0,0,0,0,-4,2u,-2lambda^3). T_p has only entries
T_p,65=T_p,75=2,T_p,76=-1. Thus N is explicit from the twenty-entry table.
Here chi1=2(u-u^3)>0. Let P0=I-ee^T/8, nu=vbar^T P0 vbar>0, and

 W_test=(chi1/nu)*P0.                                      (34)

It is genuinely PSD on R8, positive definite on e-perp, with both
required moments. This solves the FULL PSD/moment portion of the
attempt, but not the original coefficient signs.

Every REAL flow completion for this attempted W_test can be displayed.
Put R0=T0^-1,E1=W_test-N(p_test),mu=e^T vbar/8,w=vbar-mu e,nu=w^T w,

 a1=E1*e-T0^T*omega_p, b1=-E1*vbar,
 c1=b1-mu*a1, d1=c1+e*(a1^T w)/8,
 K1=(a1*e^T-e*a1^T)/8+(d1*w^T-w*d1^T)/nu,
 F=(E1+K1+J)*R0, J^T=-J, J*e=J*vbar=0,
 Y_ij=-F_ij for i!=j, Y_ii=0.                              (35)

The compatibility identities are
e^T a1=0,vbar^T b1=0,e^T b1+vbar^T a1=0.
For the first, differentiate e^T D e+omega^T T e=0 to get
e^T N e=-omega_p^T T0 e. For the second, vbar^T N vbar=chi1:
differentiate the calibrated moment, or insert
v'=-R0*T_p*vbar in v^T Dv and use
D0*vbar=-T0^T*Lbar^T*vbar/2. The mixed identity uses
T0*vbar=e and e^T omega_p=0.
These identities give e^T d1=w^T d1=0, K1e=a1,K1vbar=b1.
Consequently F e=0,F^T e=omega_p,sym(F*T0)=E1, as required.
Conversely any two skew completions differ by a skew killing e,vbar.
Thus(35) covers every real completion. Its homogeneous J has fifteen
free entries on the six-dimensional orthogonal complement. There is no
implicit matrix solve in R0: it has diagonal1, zero upper entries and
R0_ij=-hbar_j*product_(j<k<i)(1-hbar_k) for i>j. Forward substitution
in T0*x=e_j proves this formula: x_(j+1)=-hbar_j and each later value
is multiplied by 1-hbar_i.

For this attempted W_test the FIRST unsolved sign calculation is exactly

 [(E1+K1+J)*R0]_ij<=0
      for every off-diagonal ij absent from the table.     (36)

For example, put B1=E1+K1+J. The actual missing entries56 and04 require
B1_56-q*B1_57<=0 and
B1_04-q*B1_05+q*B1_06-q*u*B1_07<=0, respectively. In the first row,
N56=lambda^3+2lambda+2lambda*q+2lambda^3*q and
N57=2lambda+2lambda^3, so the non-skew contribution is exactly
E1_56-q*E1_57=u*chi1/(8nu)-lambda^3-2lambda.
These are examples from the same full list, not a replacement for it.
The thirty-six inequalities are linear in the unrestricted skew.
No claim that(36) is feasible or infeasible is made.
Choosing W_test was an attempt, not a lossless rank or shape restriction.
The full problem allows EVERY W>=0 with We=0 and(33), and uses the same
formula with E1=W-N(p). This recovers(32) with all original signs.

## 8. Exact quadratic corrections and a sufficient integration interface

A solution of(32) need not integrate. For arbitrary finite increments
p=h-hbar,Y=Lambda-Lambdabar,S=sum p_i, define

 D2(p)=diag(-p0^2,...,-p6^2,-S^2).

The EXACT full remainder, without neglected terms, is

 M=N(p)+sym(L(Y)*T0)+D2(p)+sym(L(Y)*T_p).                    (37)

Flow is exactly L(Y)^T e=omega_p. Exact signs are Y_ij>=0 at the
thirty-six zeros, and Lambdabar_ij+Y_ij>=0 at the other twenty.
Together with full PSD, (37) includes EVERY nearby word and array,
including lifts with unbounded difference quotients.

For expansions h=hbar+t*p+t^2*b+o(t^2),
Lambda=Lambdabar+t*Y+t^2*Z+o(t^2), the second remainder is

 W2=N(b)+sym(L(Z)*T0)+D2(p)+sym(L(Y)*T_p).                   (38)

Second flow is L(Z)^T e=omega_b. At a zero base entry with Y_ij=0,
sign feasibility requires Z_ij>=0. Also W2 e=0 is necessary.
If W is singular, W2 restricted to ker W must be PSD. That alone
does not ensure a truncated candidate is feasible. On e-perp decompose
range(W) plus ker(W), and write

 W=[A 0;0 0], W2=[B C;C^T D], A positive definite.

For M/t=W+tW2+O(t^2), completing the square in the upper variable
gives a lower Schur complement with leading terms

 tD-t^2 C^T A^-1 C + the lower O(t^2) term.                 (39)

The upper inverse is A^-1+O(t). A zero direction of D with an unpaid
mixed block can therefore fail at the next order; higher corrections
matter. The same issue occurs at zero leading multiplier signs.
For p_test specifically,

 D2(p_test)=diag(0,0,0,0,0,-4,-1,-1).                       (40)

An affine Lambda candidate must pay this actual negative diagonal and
the mixed term sym(L(Y)T_p). The positive moment matrix(34) alone
does not do so.

There IS a precise sufficient first-order target. Suppose(32) has
S>0, W positive definite on e-perp, and Y_ij>0 at ALL thirty-six zero
base entries. Then there is an analytic full certificate with
A>lambda^3 for all sufficiently small positive t. Here is a constructive
proof, conditional on that explicitly missing strict solution.

Set h(t)=hbar+t*p,v(t)=T(h(t))^-1 e, and

 M(t)=chi(h(t))*W/[v(t)^T W v(t)].                          (41)

The denominator at0 is chi1>0, and chi has derivative chi1.
Thus M(t)=tW+O(t^2), is PSD for small t>0, kills e exactly and has
the exact calibrated moment. For each t define
mu=e^T v/8,w=v-mu e,nu=||w||^2, and

 a=-D(h)e-T(h)^T omega(h), b=(D(h)-M(t))v,
 c=b-mu a, d=c+e*(a^T w)/8,
 K0(t)=(a e^T-e a^T)/8+(d w^T-w d^T)/nu.

All denominators stay nonzero. The identities
e^T D e+omega^T T e=0, v^T Dv=chi, and the moment of M imply the same
compatibilities as in(35), hence K0e=a,K0v=b exactly.
Let P(t)=I-ee^T/8-w(t)w(t)^T/nu(t), the analytic orthogonal projection
onto the complement of e,v(t).

Write Kbar=skew(Lbar*T0), Kdot=skew(L(Y)*T0+Lbar*T_p),
Jbar=Kbar-K0(0),Jdot=Kdot-K0'(0). Differentiating their equations gives
Jdot e=0,Jdot vbar+Jbar v'(0)=0. Since J=PJP,

 Jdot=P'(0)Jbar+Jbar P'(0)+P(0)Jdot P(0).

Therefore the skew

 J(t)=P(t)[Jbar+t*P(0)Jdot P(0)]P(t)

has value Jbar and derivative Jdot, and kills e,v(t) exactly.
Set

 L(t)=[M(t)-D(h(t))+K0(t)+J(t)]T(h(t))^-1,
 Lambda_ij(t)=-L_ij(t) off diagonal, Lambda_ii(t)=0.         (42)

Multiplication gives L(t)e=0,L(t)^T e=omega(h(t)) and the exact M(t).
Its value and derivative are Lambdabar,Y. Strict derivative signs make
all thirty-six formerly zero entries positive for small t>0; the twenty
positive entries remain so by continuity. Finally A=lambda^3+tS.
This proves the conditional integration statement with every original
check. No such strict Y,W has been found in this round.

If that strict system fails, singular W and zero first derivatives
remain possible and require(38)--(39) or higher-order work.
Failure at one chosen W/skew is not a neighborhood theorem.
Even absence of first-order capacity gain would not exclude S=0 with
sum b_i>0, or a lift with unbounded difference quotient.

## 9. Handoff and exact scope

Completed at JOINT UNVERIFIED draft level:

* Actual static equality witnesses, full directed normal checks,
  unique minima, true radii and the original eight formal coordinates.
* All-base-array rigidity: remainder ray(13), elimination by Lambda56,
  and derived full uniqueness(21), without an assumed representative.
* Uniform boundedness and convergence of arbitrary nearby arrays.
* Six explicit necessary physical tangent tests(24), handling the
  potentially illegal left continuation through(28).
* Exact slack of the older final-pair defect at the balanced point.
* A positive-capacity vector strictly surviving these contacts, its full
  PSD/moment completion, and the remaining original sign system(36).
* The full first-order system(32), exact finite quadratic model(37),
  higher-order cautions, and a conditional strict integration construction.

The FIRST unproved constructive interface is solving(32) with S>0 and
sufficient strictness for(41)--(42), or solving its singular higher-order
version. The missing negative interface is a bound A(h)<=lambda^3 on
an ACTUAL full neighborhood, covering every full certificate, zero
first-order capacity changes and nonsmooth coefficient lifts.
Neither is established. C_stat47 is an explicit contact relaxation;
C_lin47 is the explicit unresolved full linearized projection.
Neither has been identified with the physical feasible tangent cone.

No arbitrary new one-dimensional path was screened for a quick
counterexample. The vector(30) diagnoses the actual seven-coordinate
contact cone. No global seven-step, other-length or original-target
impossibility follows. Accepted5e1848ee0b153f5f would amplify an ACTUAL
above-threshold certificate; the contact vector, moment matrix and
conditional integration statement supply no such certificate.

# Two seven-step departures with the full primitive cone retained

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. Worker max2, round46,
under TASK.md and master_guidance4572ef0ed580856f; plan3be4ac482d512899.
No fact_submit is authorized or used. The original all-h/all-n Goal is OPEN.

This document screens exactly TWO physical departure constructions. It gives
no above-threshold seed. Both selected paths are excluded by explicit static
smooth convex functions, even though every global cut and the unit-quadratic
test pass at the relevant positive parameters. Their exclusion leaves all56
ordered multipliers, every original flow equation, and arbitrary PSD
remainders on the entire R8 available. Restrictions on the physical paths
are search choices, not lossless reductions of the seven-step problem.

The only mathematical predecessors are accepted facts04219554e41d9750
(the primitive identity, composition, and integer-ray results in its proof
Sections7 and9--13) and1252b8823bb5a290 (the interpolation convention).
All new algebra and all static-function constructions used here are proved
jointly. Shared drafts ff8b941536cb035e,1f135237f7524f69,268dc507a7c26073
and392fb5bb2ef7e543 were read for awareness, not used as truth premises.
No phase-cover draft, numerical record, or unverified seed is a predecessor.

## 1. Original quantities and the seven-step physical lift

For a nonnegative word h=(h_0,...,h_6), put s=sum h_i and A=1+s.
Normalize smoothness to1. Retain all eight formal gradients g_0,...,g_7,
the original positions x_i=x_0-sum_(k<i)h_k g_k, and the signed residuals

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

The optimum jet is x_star=g_star=f_star=0 when an actual witness is given.
Set

 V_h=2sum_(i<7)h_i(f_i-f_7)
       +sum_(i<7)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_7||^2.

For the full finite-node array Lambda require Lambda_ii=0 and
Lambda_ij>=0 for EVERY i!=j in {0,...,7}. Let e be the column of eight
ones and define

 T_ii=1, T_ik=h_k for k<i, T_ik=0 for k>i,
 L_flow=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_6,2s)^T,
 D_prim=diag(h_0(2-h_0),...,h_6(2-h_6),-s(s+2)),
 M_prim=D_prim+sym(L_flow T),  sym(X)=(X+X^T)/2.

The original certificate is L_flow^T e=omega and M_prim>=0 on ALL R8.
Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives, on the full original formal space,

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij
       =<M_prim,Gram(g_0,...,g_7)>.                         (1)

The last pairing is the matrix trace inner product. An actual smooth
convex trajectory with V_h<0 therefore contradicts every such certificate:
all Q_ij<=0 and all Lambda_ij>=0 would make the left side negative, while
full PSD makes the right side nonnegative. No dual-attainment assertion is
needed. Repeated ACTUAL gradients in a witness do not identify any formal
certificate coordinates.

Here is the requested lift, derived directly from the accepted integer-ray
theorem. In the accepted normalization, W_n(h) is the supremum of
2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2) over all finite-dimensional convex
L-smooth objectives with a minimizer and x_0!=x_star, under the specified
GD updates h_i/L. Write U_n^ZJ=1/B_n for the original all-split benchmark:

 C_0=B_0=1,
 C_n=max_(0<=k<n) Omega(C_k,C_(n-k-1)),
 B_n=max_(0<=k<n) G(C_k,B_(n-k-1)),
 Omega(a,c)=(a+c+sqrt((a+c)^2+4ac))/2,
 G(a,d)=2a+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8ad)/2,
 lambda=1+sqrt2.

All split indices are integers. The accepted fact proves the positive
finite limit Z_b=lim_j lambda^(-j)B_(b*2^j-1) for every integer b>=1,
and the SAME limit after any fixed nonnegative integer offset is subtracted
from the horizon. Reindexing this definition, without any phase theorem,
gives

 Z_(8b)=lim_j lambda^(-j)B_(b*2^(j+3)-1)=lambda^3 Z_b.     (2)

Conditionally suppose an actual length7 seed satisfies the full certificate
above. Replace the n primitive empty leaves of any fully expanded maximizing
B_n tree by this actual seed, retain all internal nodes, and keep its unique
terminal dominant empty leaf EMPTY. The accepted composition identities
recompute each primitive bridge as 1+d, where
d(A_left+A_right+d)=A_left*A_right, and each dominant bridge as
1+A_left/zeta, where zeta=(1+sqrt(1+8A_left/D_right))/2.

The resulting actual word Hsub_n has exactly8n physical steps and mass
Dsub_n=1+2sum Hsub_n. The general-length identities proved in the accepted
fact give

 W_(8n)(Hsub_n)=1/Dsub_n,
 [sqrt(A B_n)-(sqrt(A)-1)]^2 <= Dsub_n <= A B_n.           (3)

This is the full objective certificate, with a matching Huber witness.
For n_j=b*2^j-1 the TRUE horizon is

 N_j=8n_j=8b*2^j-8=(8b)*2^j-1-7.

Apply the accepted offset result with offset7 to its numerator. Equation(3)
implies lambda^(-j)Dsub_(n_j)->A Z_b: the difference from A B_(n_j)
is at most2(sqrt(A)-1)sqrt(A B_(n_j)), whose scaled value tends to zero.
Consequently

 lim_j B_(N_j)W_(N_j)(Hsub_(n_j))
       =Z_(8b)/(A Z_b)=lambda^3/A.                       (4)

Thus A>lambda^3 gives a fixed strict asymptotic improvement along EVERY
fixed integer b ray at its actual horizons. If epsilon=1-lambda^3/A>0,
then eventually W_(N_j)(Hsub_(n_j))<(1-epsilon/2)U_(N_j)^ZJ.
This implication supplies no seed, and is not an all-n profile theorem.

## 2. PSD coordinates do not remove the coefficient signs

We reproduce the small portion of the PSD construction needed to interpret
the two screens; it is not an invocation of draft ff8b941536cb035e.
The matrix T is invertible because it is triangular with unit diagonal.
Let v=T^(-1)e. Forward substitution gives

 v_0=1, v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i,
 chi=1-A^2 v_7^2.                                      (5)

For the actual unit quadratic f(x)=x^2/2, x_0=1, the trajectory is
x_i=g_i=v_i, f_i=v_i^2/2, every ordered residual (including optimum
residuals) is zero, and V_h=chi. To see the last assertion directly,
h_i(2-h_i)v_i^2=v_i^2-v_(i+1)^2 telescopes.

For any real flow array one has e^T M_prim e=0 and v^T M_prim v=chi.
Indeed e^T D_prim e=-sum h_i^2-s^2 and omega^T T e=sum h_i^2+s^2;
also L_flow e=0 and Tv=e. PSD forces M_prim e=0 by applying positivity
to e+t z for arbitrary real t and arbitrary column z. If h is nonzero,
e and v are independent: v=c e would have c=v_0=1 and would force every
h_i=0 in the recursion. Individual zero, unit, or subunit steps do not
cause this independence to fail for a nonzero word.

For chi>0 set P_0=I-ee^T/8, u=P_0v, nu=u^T u>0, and

 M=(chi/nu)P_0.                                        (6)

This is a rank7 PSD matrix on the full R8, with Me=0 and v^T Mv=chi.
There is an explicit REAL equality array producing it. Put

 mu=e^T v/8,
 a_sk=-D_prim e-T^T omega,
 b_sk=(D_prim-M)v,
 c_sk=b_sk-mu a_sk,
 d_sk=c_sk+e(a_sk^T u)/8,
 K_sk=(a_sk e^T-e a_sk^T)/8+(d_sk u^T-u d_sk^T)/nu,
 L=(M-D_prim+K_sk)T^(-1).                              (7)

The scalar identities above give e^T a_sk=0, v^T b_sk=0, and
e^T b_sk+v^T a_sk=0. Hence e^T d_sk=u^T d_sk=0. Multiplication now
gives K_sk e=a_sk and K_sk v=b_sk. Thus Le=0,
L^T e=omega, and D_prim+sym(LT)=M. Set Lambda_ij=-L_ij off the diagonal
and Lambda_ii=0. Since Le=0, L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda exactly.
Every original diagonal and flow equation has been respected.

One may add ANY skew J with Je=Jv=0 to K_sk in(7), and the same
identities hold. More generally the same calculation works for every
symmetric M with Me=0 and v^TMv=chi. These equations do not guarantee
Lambda_ij>=0. The witnesses below show that NO such choice of M, rank,
or homogeneous skew can make all56 signs hold on the excluded words.
This includes the fully nonzero, rank7 PSD opportunity in(6), not merely
a zero or rank-one remainder. At the first path's t=0, chi=0 and its
ordinary basic certificate has M_prim=0. No positive-rank claim is made
at that singular endpoint, and no inverse of1-h_i occurs anywhere.

## 3. Static functions, every interpolation residual, and global cuts

For any compact convex K containing0 define the single static function

 F_K(x)=max_(g in K){<x,g>-||g||^2/2}.                   (8)

Completing the square identifies its unique maximizer as the Euclidean
projection P_K(x). A proposed g in K is that projection exactly when
<x-g,z-g><=0 for all z in K. Adding this inequality at two queries
shows ||P_K(x)-P_K(y)||^2<=<P_K(x)-P_K(y),x-y>, hence the projection
is1-Lipschitz. The two maximizing inequalities also give

 0<=F_K(x+z)-F_K(x)-<P_K(x),z>
       <=<P_K(x+z)-P_K(x),z><=||z||^2.

It follows that F_K is convex, differentiable and1-smooth, with gradient
P_K. If0 is interior to K, it is the unique minimizer: F_K(0)=0, and
for x!=0 a sufficiently small positive multiple of x belongs to K and
has positive objective in(8).

At every actual query define y_i=x_i-g_i and
f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2. Direct expansion gives the complete residual
formula, in the ORIGINAL orientation,

 Q_ij=<y_j,g_i-g_j>,  including i or j equal to star.    (9)

Checking the normal against every vertex of K therefore checks every
directed finite, remote, terminal and optimum comparison. In particular
Q_(i,star)=0; the other optimum direction is also included because0 is in K.
The norm telescope along actual GD is

 ||x_i||^2-||x_(i+1)||^2
       =2h_i f_i+h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2.

Using the actual terminal value gives

 V_h=||x_0||^2-||x_7+s g_7||^2.                        (10)

In particular, if x_7=g_7 the second square is A^2||g_7||^2.

For reference, the original global-cut screen at index j is

 A(h_j-1)_+ <= P_j^+ D_j^+,
 P_j^+=1+sum_(i<j)h_i, D_j^+=1+sum_(i>j)h_i.           (11)

Here these superscript-plus quantities include1, to distinguish them from
unshifted prefix/suffix sums in other project findings. To derive(11) when
h_j>1, set eta=(h_j-1)/D_j^+ and K=[-eta,1]. Start at x_0=P_j^+.
All queries through j have gradient1; query j equals1. Its step lands at
-D_j^+ eta. Every suffix gradient is-eta, and the terminal equals-eta.
This uses one static function(8), with unique minimizer0 and true initial
distance P_j^+. Equation(10) gives V_h=(P_j^+)^2-A^2 eta^2, proving
necessity. At h_j<=1 the displayed screen is automatically satisfied.

## 4. Departure I: a nonzero-moment path out of the balanced word

Write r2=sqrt2, u2=r2-1=1/lambda. For0<=t<=1/100 set

 a=r2-t, b=2+3t, c=a-1=u2-t, B=b-1=1+3t,
 h^I(t)=(a,b,a,1+lambda,a,b,a),
 P=1+2a+b=lambda^2+t,
 A=lambda^3+2t=2P+lambda.                              (12)

This is one selected symmetric physical path. Its equality of entries and
its fixed middle step are not restrictions on any competing certificate.
At t=0 the actual word is obtained by three levels of the accepted balanced
primitive join: singleton sqrt2, three-step(sqrt2,2,sqrt2), then(12).
Its capacity is lambda^3 and the embedded zero remainders remain zero.

For every t>0 in the displayed interval its capacity exceeds lambda^3.
Nevertheless it is excluded in the ENTIRE original cone, as shown below.

### 4.1 Both cheap screens pass strictly

For the center j=3, both shifted side sums in(11) equal P, and

 P^2-lambda A=2(lambda^2-lambda)t+t^2>0.

Every step equal to a has a<3/2 and A>14. Its shifted side sums satisfy
P_j^+D_j^+>=P_j^++D_j^+-1=A-a>A/2>A(a-1).
At either step b, the smaller side sum exceeds2 and the other exceeds10:
lambda-t>2 and A-a-b>14-3/2-203/100>10. Thus their product exceeds20,
whereas A(b-1)<15*(103/100)<20. This checks every index of this word.

The absolute unit-quadratic terminal product is lambda c^4 B^2. At t=0,
A lambda c^4 B^2=1. Its logarithmic derivative for0<=t<=1/100 is

 2/A-4/c+6/B <1/7-8+6=-13/7<0,

using A>14, 0<c<1/2, B>=1. Hence A|v_7|<1 and chi>0 whenever t>0.
In particular a rank7 REAL equality remainder(6) exists; signs remain
the essential condition, rather than the unit-quadratic moment.

### 4.2 One exact quadrilateral and all eight actual queries

Define

 R2=lambda(1-t-t^2/2)/(lambda-t)
       =[c lambda^2+(lambda/2)(2-a^2)]/(a+1),
 U=(1,0), V=(-1/2,sqrt(R2-1/4)),
 G4=-(lambda U+V)/a,
 K=conv{U,G4,V,-B V}.                                  (13)

The two expressions for R2 agree because lambda u2=1, a+1=lambda-t,
and2-a^2=2sqrt2*t-t^2. We have R2<=1, with equality only at t=0.
For a useful lower bound, since7/5<sqrt2<3/2,

 R2=1-[(1-u2)t+t^2/2]/(1-u2 t)
       >1-(61/100)t >=9939/10000>1/4.                  (14)

Indeed (1-u2)+t/2<3/5+1/200 and1-u2t>199/200; their ratio is less
than121/199<61/100. Thus U,V are independent. The positive relation
lambda U+a G4+V=0 makes0 interior to their triangle, hence interior to K.
Function(8) consequently has unique minimizer0.

Start at x_0=P U. The actual queries and gradients are

 x_i=(1+sum_(k=i)^2 h_k)U, g_i=U, 0<=i<=3,
 x_4=-lambda U,             g_4=G4,
 x_5=V,                    g_5=V,
 x_6=-B V,                 g_6=-B V,
 x_7=c B V,                g_7=c B V.                  (15)

An empty sum in the first line is zero. Every GD equality follows directly
from(12)--(13); in particular -lambda U-a G4=V. Since0<cB<103/200<1,
the terminal belongs to the segment from0 to V and therefore to K.
The true initial distance is exactly P, not a surrogate radius.

Here is the full normal check. For0<=i<=3, y_i is a nonnegative multiple
of U, and

 <U,G4>=-(lambda-1/2)/a<0,
 <U,V>=-1/2,
 <U,-B V>=B/2<=103/200<1=<U,U>.

So every prefix normal supports all vertices. The only other nonzero
normal is y_4=(-c lambda U+V)/a. Its product with V-G4 is

 [-c lambda^2+lambda(2-a^2)<U,V>+(a+1)R2]/a^2=0.       (16)

Use this equality to compare the remaining vertices:

 <y_4,U-G4>=<y_4,U-V>
       =[-3c lambda/2-1/2-R2]/a<0,
 <y_4,-B V-G4>=-(B+1)<y_4,V>
       =-(B+1)(R2+c lambda/2)/a<0.

The normal comparison with G4 itself is zero. At5,6,7 the normals are
zero because x_i=g_i. This proves every query in(15) is a projection onto
the SAME static K. Formula(9) is the exact value of every one of its
original interpolation residuals; all56 finite directed residuals and all
optimum residuals have the required sign. No terminal or remote row is
missing. Values are explicitly f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.

### 4.3 A finite strict negative-energy bound, not just a derivative

Equation(10) gives the exact energy

 V_(h^I(t))=P^2-A^2 c^2 B^2 R2.                         (17)

For0<t<=1/100 set X=(A/P)cB. Since A/P=lambda-u2*t/P,

 X=1+(3-lambda)t-3lambda t^2-(u2*t/P)cB.

Use lambda<5/2, P>5, u2<1/2, cB<3/5, and t<=1/100 to obtain

 X>1+(1/2-3/40-3/50)t
     =1+(73/200)t>1+(9/25)t.

Combining this with(14), all factors being positive, yields

 X^2 R2>(1+(18/25)t)(1-(61/100)t)
        =1+(11/100)t-(549/1250)t^2
        >1+t/10.

The last strict inequality follows from t<=1/100, since
11/100-549/125000=13201/125000>1/10. Therefore

 V_(h^I(t))<-P^2 t/10<0,  EVERY0<t<=1/100.              (18)

At t=0 the same actual construction has energy exactly zero, R2=1,
chi=0, and the center cut is an equality. All formulas stay defined there.
The positive interval passes BOTH cheap screens but has a negative full
primitive energy. Thus no choice among all56 coefficients and no nonzero
or higher-rank PSD remainder can certify ANY point of this interval.

## 5. Departure II: an asymmetric subunit path from a full-rank remainder

The second, genuinely different physical construction is the affine path

 h^II(t)=(1+t/2, 1+3t/2, 1-t/20, 1+23t/10,
           1+t/2, 1+3t/2, 1+4t/5),  0<=t<=1,
 A(t)=8+(141/20)t.                                     (19)

For t>0, the third step is subunit, all steps are positive, and the word
is asymmetric. At t=0 it is the all-ones word. We first give its ACTUAL
full-rank starting certificate rather than assuming feasibility.

### 5.1 The exact starting array and complete PSD remainder

For i=0,...,6 set

 Lambda_(i,i+1)=2(i+1)^2,
 Lambda_(i+1,i)=2(i+1)(i+2),

and set every other off-diagonal entry and every diagonal to zero.
These specify ALL56 entries. At node0 the outflow minus inflow is-2;
at each node i=1,...,6 it is
2[(i+1)^2+i(i+1)-(i+1)(i+2)-i^2]=-2;
at node7 it is112-98=14. Thus the original flow holds for h_i=1.

For a unit step the quadratic part of Q_(i+1,i) is
(||g_i||^2+||g_(i+1)||^2)/2, and

 Q_(i,i+1)+Q_(i+1,i)=||g_(i+1)||^2-<g_i,g_(i+1)>.

After adding2sum_(i=0)^6(i+1)Q_(i+1,i) to V_h, the value terms cancel,
and the quadratic is
sum_(i=0)^6(i+1)(||g_i||^2+||g_(i+1)||^2)-56||g_7||^2.
Add2sum_(i=0)^6(i+1)^2[Q_(i,i+1)+Q_(i+1,i)]. The coefficient of g_0^2
is1; for1<=i<=6 it is2i+1+2i^2=i^2+(i+1)^2; at7 it is-49+98=49.
Every cross coefficient is-2(i+1)^2. Hence the ENTIRE remainder is

 V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij
       =sum_(i=0)^6(i+1)^2||g_i-g_(i+1)||^2.             (20)

It is PSD on R8, has rank7 and kernel exactly the common-gradient line.
The starting support is only a concrete initialization; it is not imposed
on any later certificate in our exclusion of the path.

### 5.2 Every global cut and the unit-quadratic screen along the path

Here |v_7|=(207/4000)t^7, so throughout0<=t<=1,

 A|v_7| <= (301/20)(207/4000)=62307/80000<1.             (21)

In particular chi>0 everywhere, including the unit-step endpoint t=0.
No zero terminal product is incorrectly inverted.

For each superunit coordinate, the exact cut margin
P_j^+D_j^+-A(h_j-1) is in the following table. Index2 has h_2<=1 and
thus passes its cut without this formula.

 j | exact margin
 --|------------------------------------------------------
 0 | 7+(51/20)t-(141/40)t^2
 1 | 12+(11/10)t-(161/20)t^2
 3 | 16+(3/5)t-(2151/200)t^2
 4 | 15+(81/4)t+(25/4)t^2
 5 | 12+(23/10)t-(271/40)t^2
 6 | 7-(3/20)t-(141/25)t^2

Each row except4 is concave in t and hence at least the lesser of its
two endpoint values on[0,1]. The values at1 for rows0,1,3,5,6 are,
respectively,241/40,101/20,1169/200,301/40,121/100, all positive;
their values at0 are positive as displayed. Row4 is at least15.
Consequently EVERY original global cut holds strictly along this path.

### 5.3 The complete last-pair static witness

For17/20<=t<=1 define

 P=1+sum_(i<5)h_i=6+(19/4)t,
 B=h_5-1=(3/2)t, C=h_6-1=(4/5)t,
 U_tail=B+C+2=A-P,
 H_def=BC[1+B(P+A)/P^2]-1.                              (22)

In this interval P>10, P<11, P+A>=2403/100>24, B>=51/40>5/4, and
BC>=867/1000. Therefore

 H_def>(867/1000)(1+30/121)-1
       =9917/121000>2/25.                              (23)

First suppose BC<1. Set

 kappa=B^2 C+U_tail(1-BC)>0,
 tau_tri=B^2 C/kappa in(0,1),
 u=(1,0), w=(tau_tri,sqrt(tau_tri(1-tau_tri))),
 v=-(B u+w)/(1+C),  K=conv{u,v,w}.                     (24)

The positive relation B u+(1+C)v+w=0 and independence of u,w put0
strictly inside the triangle. Its static function(8) has unique minimizer0.
Start at x_0=P u. All original prefix queries0,...,5 have gradient u and
positions x_i=(1+sum_(k=i)^4 h_k)u. The remaining actual data are

 x_6=-B u, g_6=v;    x_7=w, g_7=w.                    (25)

The last update holds because -B u-(1+C)v=w. The prefix includes the
original subunit step h_2; none of its queries is omitted. The true initial
distance is P. Give every query its actual value x_i dot g_i-||g_i||^2/2.

Prefix normals are nonnegative multiples of u, and compare negatively
with w-u and v-u because <u,w>=tau_tri<1 and
<u,v>=-(B+tau_tri)/(1+C)<0. At6, y_6=(-BC u+w)/(1+C). Direct expansion
using <u,w>=||w||^2=tau_tri gives

 <y_6,w-v>=(-B^2 C+kappa*tau_tri)/(1+C)^2=0,
 <y_6,u-v>=-BC(1-tau_tri)/(1+C)<0.                     (26)

The coefficient of tau_tri in the first numerator is
B+1+C+1-BC(1+C+1)=U_tail-BC(C+2)=kappa;
the equality to kappa follows also by expanding(24). The terminal normal
is zero. Thus these are projections onto ONE static K. Equation(9) supplies
ALL56 directed finite residuals and all optimum comparisons with their
actual nonpositive signs.

Using(10), the exact primitive energy is

 V_h=P^2-A^2 tau_tri=-U_tail*P^2*H_def/kappa.            (27)

For an algebra check,
A^2 B^2 C-P^2 kappa
=U_tail{BC[P^2+B(P+A)]-P^2}.
Furthermore kappa=U_tail-BC(C+2)<U_tail, so(23) and P>10 imply

 V_h<-P^2 H_def<-8.                                    (28)

If BC>=1, including the singular boundary BC=1, instead use the scalar
static function F(x)=x^2/2 for x<=1 and F(x)=x-1/2 for x>=1.
Its derivative min(x,1) is continuous, nondecreasing and1-Lipschitz;
its unique minimizer is0. Start at x_0=P. The original prefix through5
has gradient1 and query5 equals1. Query6 is-B with gradient-B, and
query7 is BC>=1 with gradient1. Every query is an actual query of this
ONE function. Their values again equal x_i g_i-g_i^2/2. Formula(9) is
valid by expansion: the only nonzero normals are nonnegative scalars
at gradients1, which support every actual gradient and the optimum0.
Thus all ordered residuals, including terminal and optimum ones, have
the correct sign. Equation(10) now gives

 V_h=P^2-[P+(1+B)(1+C)]^2<-21<-8,                      (29)

because P>10 and(1+B)(1+C)>1. The true initial distance remains P.
No degenerate triangle is used at BC=1, and no formal coordinate is
removed or identified at that boundary.

Together(28)--(29) give an exact strict original-cone obstruction for
EVERY17/20<=t<=1. Before that interval the capacity is at most

 A(17/20)=5597/400<14<lambda^3,

whereas A(1)=301/20>lambda^3. Therefore every point on the WHOLE selected
path(19) with capacity above lambda^3 lies in the excluded interval.
The full-rank initialization and the passing screens do not reach a seed.

## 6. What these two obstructions leave open

The first construction genuinely crosses from chi=0 into chi>0, while
increasing physical capacity above lambda^3. The second starts with the
explicit full-rank original certificate(20), permits a subunit physical
step, is asymmetric, and reaches capacity301/20. In both cases the complete
static witnesses prohibit every possible original nonnegative coefficient
array, including all homogeneous skew repairs and full nonzero PSD
remainders. No prescribed rank-one rate mechanism was reused.

These are restrictions on TWO declared physical families only. They do not
prove full seven-step impossibility, a maximum-capacity theorem, or the
original all-h/all-n asymptotic profile. A subthreshold starting certificate
and the conditional lift(4) are not substitutes for the missing seed.

A specific direction outside the exclusions remains: vary the two ordered
three-step blocks independently together with the central bridge, break
the first path's repeated leaves and equal side peaks, and adjust both
terminal steps so that the last-pair defect(22) is not positive. For example,
the seven independent physical coordinates can be written as
(sqrt2+p0,2+p1,sqrt2+p2,1+lambda+p3,sqrt2+p4,2+p5,sqrt2+p6), subject only
to nonnegativity and actual screens. This is a remaining coordinate domain,
not a third construction attempted here and not a claimed feasible family.
It must still solve all56 signs with full PSD; neither draft supplies this.

All algebra above was derived textually. No parameter scan, numerical
evaluation, solver, symbolic-evaluation program, benchmark replay, formal
verification, installation, consult, or native subagent was used.
The ONE assigned round's two-construction allowance is consumed. It is
not renewed by this handoff. No process-exit or external counter observation
is asserted. Every new result in this document remains UNVERIFIED.
